### [1] [Medical Image Alignment Assessment as a Test of Generalist Visual Reasoning in Frontier Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06896)
*Ross Callaghan,Niannu Gao,Hojjat Azadbakht,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以医学图像配准评估为测试场景，考察前沿多模态大模型（MLLMs）能否在无任务特定优化下完成新颖的视觉判断。结果显示，前沿MLLMs正到达一个拐点，GPT-6在几乎所有测试场景中准确率超85%，而数月前发布的模型表现接近随机；本地微调模型在特定任务上可媲美甚至超越前沿模型，但迁移到未见场景时效果明显更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿MLLMs被寄予成为通用视觉推理器的期望，但关键在于它们能否在无需任务特定参数优化的情况下完成可靠的新颖视觉判断。医学图像配准评估是理想测试对象：人类视觉评估虽为金标准，却需训练有素的操作人员，难以扩展到大规模数据集。因此需要检验MLLMs在此类任务上的通用性，并比较与需任务特定优化的本地小模型之间的权衡。

Method: 评估近期几代MLLMs在两个典型医学图像配准任务上的表现，变化了多种提示策略和图像呈现方式；同时与本地微调的MLLM及任务特定CNN进行对比，以考察前沿通用模型与需特定优化但可本地部署的较小模型之间的权衡。

Result: 模型表现正到达拐点：几个月前发布的模型泛化能力差，在某些设置下仅略高于随机水平；而GPT-6在几乎所有测试场景中准确率超过85%。微调本地模型在其训练任务上可达到或超过前沿模型表现，但向未见场景的迁移效果明显更差。

Conclusion: 医学图像配准可作为通用视觉推理的有用测试平台。前沿多模态模型正开始获得可作为异构医学影像流程通用质量控制机制的能力。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly positioned as general purpose visual reasoners as part of the quest for artificial general intelligence. A key test of this generality is whether they can perform novel visual judgments that humans can make reliably from visual evidence and task instructions, without task-specific parameter optimisation. We investigate this question through the task of medical image alignment assessment, where the goal is to establish whether there is anatomical correspondence between two images. Human visual assessment of image alignment is still the gold standard and most common approach; however, it requires trained operators and is impractical to scale for large datasets. We evaluate recent generations of MLLMs on two exemplar medical image alignment tasks, varying both prompting strategies and image-presentation methods. We compare against a locally fine-tuned MLLM and a task-specific CNN to examine the trade-off between frontier general purpose models and smaller models that require specific task optimisation but can be used locally. We show that are reaching an inflection point, where frontier MLLMs can now perform effective visual assessment of medical image alignment. Models released only a few months ago generalise poorly and, in some settings, perform barely above chance, whereas GPT-6 achieves over 85% across almost all scenarios tested. Fine-tuned local models can match or exceed frontier-model performance on the tasks on which they are trained, but transfer substantially less effectively to unseen settings. These findings identify medical image alignment as a useful test bed for generalist visual reasoning and suggest that frontier multimodal models are beginning to acquire capabilities that could support a common quality-control mechanism across heterogeneous medical-imaging pipelines.

</details>


### [2] [RADC: Risk-Aware Dual Caching for Vision-Language Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06932)
*Siyu Huang,Yueyong Chen,Xuejiao Li,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RADC，通过可靠的双缓存机制增强原型学习，解决视觉语言模型测试时适应中的背景偏置和熵缓存准入不可靠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于缓存的视觉语言模型测试时适应（TTA）受两大问题制约：全局表示中存在背景偏置，以及表示变化下基于熵的缓存准入不可靠。

Method: RADC包含三部分：(1) 语义前景缓存，从CLIP表示中聚合类别一致的空间证据，生成前景原型以补充全局缓存并减轻背景干扰；(2) 高斯风险准入，将多视图表示建模为对角高斯分布，联合考虑类分离度与特征不确定性以筛选可靠缓存候选；(3) 融合零样本logits与全局、前景缓存预测进行鲁棒推理。

Result: 在跨域和分布外基准上的大量实验表明，RADC持续取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: RADC通过双缓存与高斯风险准入有效提升视觉语言模型测试时适应的鲁棒性，达到SOTA。

Abstract: Cache-based test-time adaptation (TTA) for vision-language models is often hindered by background bias in global representations and unreliable entropy-based cache admission under representation variations. To address these limitations, we propose RADC, which enhances prototype learning through reliable dual caching. RADC introduces a Semantic Foreground Cache that aggregates category-consistent spatial evidence from CLIP representations, yielding foreground prototypes that complement the global cache while mitigating background interference. To reliably manage both caches, Gaussian Risk Admission models multi-view representations as diagonal Gaussian distributions and jointly considers class separation and feature uncertainty to prioritize reliable cache candidates. RADC integrates zero-shot logits with complementary global- and foreground-cache predictions for robust inference. Extensive experiments on cross-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks demonstrate consistent state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [3] [UniPro: Unified Multi-Mode Medical Image Segmentation from 2D Images to 3D Volumes via Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06938)
*Bangwei Guo,Yunhe Gao,Meng Ye,Yang Zhou,Difei Gu,Guoning Zhang,Leon Axel,Dimitris Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniPro是一个统一的医学图像分割模型，通过"传播"机制将2D分割扩展到3D体数据，在单一slice框架内支持语义、上下文、交互和传播式分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割在两个方面割裂：一是分割范式（语义/上下文/交互），二是数据维度（2D/3D）。这些方法通常各自独立开发，但在临床中工作流是多形态的，且无论以何种方式启动，细粒度修正自然在2D视图上进行，而体数据需要将2D编辑一致传播到整个体。

Method: 核心洞察：体传播与上下文分割共享相同的"参考条件预测"机制，区别仅在于参考图像-掩码对来自其他病例还是相邻切片。基于此，UniPro在单一slice框架内，以类别先验、参考样例、用户点击、邻片预测作为模式特化条件输入，支持四种分割。并引入双向与3D监督，超越逐slice损失以提升传播可靠性。

Result: 在多种模态和解剖结构上的大量实验表明，UniPro在所有分割设置下均取得强劲性能，能从稀疏2D初始化实现标注高效的3D分割，并减少逐slice修正的工作量。

Conclusion: UniPro统一了分割范式与数据维度，通过传播机制实现了标注高效、交互友好的3D医学图像分割。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains fragmented along two axes: segmentation paradigms and data dimensionality. Existing methods are typically developed separately for semantic, in-context, and interactive segmentation, and are further specialized to either native 2D images or 3D volumetric data. In clinical practice, however, segmentation workflows take many forms: a case may be initialized by semantic prediction, reference-guided segmentation, or user interaction. Regardless of how it begins, fine-grained refinement is naturally performed on 2D views; for volumetric scans, such 2D edits must propagate coherently to the rest of the volume. We present UniPro, a unified model that bridges segmentation paradigms and data dimensionality, using propagation to extend 2D segmentation to 3D volumes. Our key insight is that volumetric propagation and in-context segmentation share the same reference-conditioned prediction mechanism, differing only in whether the reference image-mask pairs come from other cases or from previously segmented neighboring slices. Building on this view, UniPro supports semantic, in-context, interactive, and propagation-based segmentation within a single slice-based framework, using class priors, reference exemplars, user clicks, and neighboring-slice predictions as mode-specific conditioning inputs. To improve propagation reliability, UniPro further incorporates bidirectional and 3D supervision to regularize slice-wise propagation beyond per-slice losses. Extensive experiments across diverse modalities and anatomies show that UniPro achieves strong performance across all segmentation settings, enabling annotation-efficient 3D segmentation from sparse 2D initialization and reducing slice-by-slice correction effort.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond the Linear Representation Hypothesis: Non-Linear Activation Steering in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06945)
*Muhammad Atif Butt,Paweł Skierś,Joost Van De Weijer,Kamil Deja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KANSteer，用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络将概念遍历建模为曲线，证明T2I模型中概念表示偏离线性方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表示假设（LRH）认为高层概念在激活空间中被编码为线性方向，但许多视觉概念的自然过渡并非线性：如晴朗到暴风雨之间存在中间天气状态，毛毛虫到蝴蝶的演变也不是翅膀逐渐长大的过程。作者质疑这些真实中间状态是否也被模型非线性地表示。

Method: 作者提出KANSteer，将概念遍历建模为穿过中间状态的曲线。采用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KANs）提供一个一维坐标，其学习到的函数定义轨迹，使引导方向沿概念变化，同时保持可解释的表示。

Result: 在多个概念和文本到图像扩散transformer上，激活轨迹明显偏离直线，KANSteer比线性引导能更贴近并更平滑地遍历中间属性。

Conclusion: T2I模型对概念的表征是非线性的，KANSteer提供了更优的可解释概念引导方法，挑战了LRH在视觉概念上的适用性。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often relies on the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH), which assumes that high-level concepts are encoded as linear directions in activation space. Yet a natural visual concept does not necessarily require a linear visual transition: between sunny and stormy lies an intermediate weather state such as a sky with a few white clouds, not simply a weaker storm; between a caterpillar and a butterfly, the progression is not a caterpillar with continuously growing wings. This raises the question of whether such true intermediate states are also represented nonlinearly by the model. Indeed, when we prompt text-to-image models directly for intermediate attributes, their activations rarely fall along the straight direction connecting the endpoints. Therefore, we propose KANSteer, which models concept traversal as a curve passing through its intermediate states. Seeking a representation that is both simple and interpretable, we propose to use Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), which provide a one-dimensional coordinate whose learned functions define the trajectory. This allows the steering direction to vary along the concept while preserving an interpretable representation. Across several concepts and text-to-image diffusion transformers, we find that their activation trajectories substantially deviate from straight lines, and that KANSteer provide a closer fit and smoother traversal of intermediate attributes than linear steering.

</details>


### [5] [DistScene: Object-to-Scene Distillation for 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06960)
*Kunming Luo,Hongyu Yan,Ken Deng,Chengcheng Zhou,Tianyu Liu,Haipeng Li,Haibin Huang,Xuelong Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DistScene框架，通过联合建模环境与物体实现单图像组合式3D场景生成，提升场景级空间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将场景主要表示为物体集合，忽略了环境作为几何上下文对物体摆放的作用，导致空间一致性不足。

Method: 包含三个核心设计：(1) Scene-Frame Generation，在共享坐标系中联合生成环境与物体组件，学习其几何与相对摆放；(2) Object-Centric Refinement，在局部坐标系中结合场景上下文精炼各物体；(3) Object-to-Scene Distillation，通过自动合成渲染的合成场景，将预训练物体生成先验迁移到场景生成。

Result: 在室内与室外基准上评估，相比基线在场景级空间一致性上取得提升。

Conclusion: 将环境显式建模为场景组件并迁移物体先验，可有效改善单图像3D场景生成的空间一致性。

Abstract: We present DistScene, a framework for single-image compositional 3D scene generation by jointly modeling the environment and individual objects. Unlike existing methods that represent scenes primarily as collections of objects, we model the environment as an explicit scene component to provide geometric context for object placement. Specifically, we introduce Scene-Frame Generation, which jointly generates separate environment and object components in a shared coordinate frame, allowing their geometry and relative placement to be learned together. Then we introduce Object-Centric Refinement to refine each object in a local frame with scene context. Finally, we develop Object-to-Scene Distillation to transfer pretrained object-generation priors to scene generation through automatically composed and rendered synthetic scenes. Evaluations on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate improved scene-level spatial coherence over the evaluated baselines. Project page: https://coolbeam.github.io/DistScene/

</details>


### [6] [BoT-Feedback: Grounding Multimodal Reasoning in Biomechanical Evidence for Explainable Human Action Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06972)
*Xu Dong,Wanqing Li,Anthony Adeyemi-Ejeye,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BoT-Feedback框架，将MLLM推理锚定于结构化生物力学证据，实现可解释的人类动作反馈生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在动作反馈生成上直接从视觉观测推断反馈，产生泛泛建议、可解释性差且存在物理上不合理的幻觉。而专业教练通过关节运动学、姿态和身体动力学的显式生物力学推理来诊断表现，因此需要将MLLM推理建立于结构化生物力学证据之上。

Method: 核心贡献是Biomechanics of Thought (BoT)四阶段推理框架：识别动作、定位关键身体区域、分析专家与学生表现间的定量生物力学差异、合成可解释教练反馈。配套开发即插即用的Biomechanical Data Parser (BDP)将视频转为结构化生物力学描述符，并提出时间对齐策略匹配专家与学生动作。还构建了BiomAF基准，包含师生配对视频、3D骨架、生物力学属性和专家教练标注。

Result: 在12个开源与闭源MLLM上的实验表明，将推理锚定于生物力学证据可持续提升反馈质量、可解释性和鲁棒性，并大幅减少生物力学幻觉。BoT-Feedback将平均专家评分从2.07提升至2.95（+40%）。

Conclusion: 基于生物力学证据的推理使紧凑开源MLLM在可解释动作反馈生成上接近更大规模专有系统的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in visual understanding and multimodal reasoning, yet they remain fundamentally limited in Human Action Feedback Generation. Existing methods infer coaching feedback directly from visual observations, producing generic advice, limited interpretability, and physically implausible hallucinations. In contrast, expert human coaches diagnose performance through explicit biomechanical reasoning over joint kinematics, posture, and body dynamics. We introduce BoT-Feedback, a framework that grounds MLLM reasoning in structured biomechanical evidence. Our key contribution is Biomechanics of Thought (BoT), a four-stage reasoning framework that progressively identifies the action, localises the critical body regions, analyses quantitative biomechanical differences between expert and student performances, and synthesises interpretable coaching feedback. To support this reasoning process, we develop a plug-and-play Biomechanical Data Parser (BDP) that converts videos into structured biomechanical descriptors and an alignment strategy that temporally matches expert and student motions. We further introduce BiomAF, a benchmark containing paired teacher-student videos, 3D skeletons, biomechanical attributes, and expert-coaching annotations. Experiments across twelve open- and closed-source MLLMs demonstrate that grounding reasoning in biomechanical evidence consistently improves feedback quality, interpretability, and robustness while substantially reducing biomechanical hallucinations. BoT-Feedback improves the average expert evaluation score from 2.07 to 2.95 (+40%), enabling compact open-source MLLMs to approach the performance of substantially larger proprietary systems for explainable action feedback generation.

</details>


### [7] [Event Cameras for Melt-Pool Monitoring in Additive Manufacturing: A Benchmark and a Cross-Machine Transfer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06973)
*Mohamad Yazan Sadoun,Sarah Sharif,Yingtao Liu,Zahed Siddique,Yaser Mike Banad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynAM-E，首个金属增材制造熔池监测的公开事件相机基准，含85个物理校准事件分片、跨8机构15来源，附基线与跨机评估划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔池监测对金属增材制造合格认证至关重要，但该领域缺乏公开的事件相机基准；事件相机以微秒级时间分辨率、低数据率捕捉亮度变化，契合AM瞬态需求。

Method: 构建多源仿真事件相机基准SynAM-E（85个物理校准事件分片、15来源、8机构），提供公开基线与固定跨机评估划分；开展单机案例、NIST AMMT构建的近传感器事件率计数，以及紧凑脉冲神经网络（93k参数）实验，并设计针对相机身份捷径的信任测试。

Result: 单机事件空间监测准确率与稠密帧相当（macro-F1 0.874 vs 0.863）；强度融合仅提升+0.006；近传感器计数器以约1/380读出量恢复528.7 Hz振荡；脉冲模型推理能耗低15倍，macro-F1仅降0.073；过程类型分类通过信任测试，材料分类受相机波段混淆。

Conclusion: SynAM-E为金属AM熔池监测提供首个公开事件相机基准，验证事件相机在低数据率、低能耗下的有效性，并揭示材料分类中的相机波段混淆这一语料结构局限。

Abstract: Melt-pool monitoring is central to qualifying metal additive manufacturing (AM), yet no public event-camera benchmark exists for this domain. Event cameras report per-pixel brightness changes with microsecond timing instead of reading full frames, giving the temporal resolution AM transients demand at a fraction of the data rate. We present SynAM-E (Synthetic AM Events), the first public multi-source simulated event-camera benchmark for metal-AM melt-pool monitoring: 85 physics-calibrated event shards from 15 sources across 8 institutions, with public baselines and fixed cross-machine evaluation splits. On a single-machine case study, event-spatial monitoring matches dense-frame accuracy (0.874 versus 0.863 macro-F1), and the absolute intensity that events discard adds only +0.006 under fusion. On the NIST Additive Manufacturing Metrology Testbed (AMMT) build, a near-sensor event-rate counter recovers a raw-frame-confirmed 528.7 Hz intensity oscillation at ~380 times less sensor readout than the frame stream requires. A compact 93 k-parameter spiking model runs at 15 times lower modeled inference energy for a 0.073 macro-F1 cost. Every cross-source task includes a built-in trust test against camera identity shortcuts: process-type classification passes while material classification remains confounded by camera band, a corpus-structural limitation the release documents and the trust test exposes.

</details>


### [8] [Visual-Invariance-Augmented Feature Optimal Alignment for Transferable Adversarial Attacks against Closed-Source MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06977)
*Xiaojun Jia,Simeng Qin,Yiming Li,Jie Liao,Sensen Gao,Ke Ma,Yang Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IAU-FOA攻击，通过全局-局部特征对齐、自适应非平衡最优传输与视觉不变性增强，提升对闭源MLLM的定向迁移攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向迁移攻击仅用全局图像级特征（如[CLS]嵌入）对齐对抗样本与目标样本，忽略patch级视觉结构，导致跨异构闭源MLLM迁移性差。

Method: 全局用余弦目标缩小语义差距；局部将patch token聚类为紧凑模式，用最优传输做细粒度匹配；引入置信度自适应非平衡传输，对弱匹配簇放松质量约束；并提出视觉不变性增强（双向像素强度缩放、逐通道白平衡）模拟曝光/对比度/光照/色温变化。

Result: 在开源与闭源MLLM上的大量实验表明，IAU-FOA持续优于当前最先进的迁移攻击方法。

Conclusion: 全局-局部特征对齐结合自适应非平衡传输与视觉不变性增强，能有效提升对闭源MLLM的定向迁移攻击能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain vulnerable to transferable adversarial examples, especially in black-box settings where only open-source surrogate models are accessible. Existing targeted transfer attacks mainly align adversarial and target samples using global image-level features, such as encoder [CLS] embeddings. However, such coarse alignment insufficiently exploits patch-level visual structures, limiting transferability across heterogeneous closed-source MLLMs. We propose IAU-FOA, a visual-invariance-augmented feature optimal alignment attack with adaptive unbalanced transport, to improve targeted transferability against closed-source MLLMs. IAU-FOA aligns adversarial and target samples at both global and local levels: a cosine-based objective narrows their global semantic gap, while patch tokens are clustered into compact local patterns and matched through optimal transport for fine-grained feature alignment. Balanced optimal transport enforces fixed marginal masses even for local clusters without reliable counterparts, potentially introducing misleading alignment gradients. We therefore introduce confidence-adaptive unbalanced transport to relax these constraints for weakly matched clusters, aiming to reduce unreliable local alignment and improve adversarial transferability. We further study the effect of input transformations and propose visual-invariance augmentation, which applies bidirectional pixel-intensity rescaling and per-channel white-balance adjustment to simulate exposure, contrast, illumination, and color-temperature variations. This strategy encourages adversarial perturbations to generalize across different visual encoders. Extensive experiments on open-source and closed-source MLLMs show that IAU-FOA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art transferable attack methods. Code is available at https://github.com/jiaxiaojunQAQ/IAU-FOA.

</details>


### [9] [Hierarchy-GBP: Accelerating Factor Graph Inference via Abstraction and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06978)
*Yuzhou Cheng,Tom Yates,Ignacio Alzugaray,Danyal Akarca,Pedro A. M. Mediano,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出H-GBP，一种两阶段迭代框架，先通过粗图抽象解决全局误差再投影回原图，然后用标准GBP细化局部误差，从而加速高斯信念传播收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBP是分布式推断算法，适合可扩展空间智能，但实验发现它仅在局部高效：能快速平滑邻居变量间剧烈变化的局部消息误差，而跨图区域的全局误差只能通过长程消息传播逐步修正，导致收敛慢。

Method: 提出Hierarchy-GBP（H-GBP）：迭代两阶段框架，先用粗图近似（abstraction）解决全局误差并投影回原图（recovery），再用GBP细化剩余局部误差。通过推导abstraction与recovery结合矩阵算子并分析其谱半径，证明收敛到最优解。

Result: 在线性稀疏图上实验显示H-GBP从根本上比标准GBP收敛更快；在Pose Graph Optimization（PGO）和Bundle Adjustment（BA）上验证，显著加速大规模PGO，并在所有测试BA尺度上达到SOTA运行时间。

Conclusion: H-GBP通过分离全局与局部误差修正，实现了比标准GBP更快的收敛，并在大规模空间推断任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) is a distributed inference algorithm that passes messages in graphical models, making it attractive for scalable spatial intelligence. However, we find GBP most effective locally: it rapidly smooths message errors that vary sharply between neighbor variables, but corrects global errors across distant graph regions incrementally through long-range message propagations. We propose Hierarchy-GBP (H-GBP), an iterative, two-stage framework that accelerates GBP by first solving these global errors with a coarse graph approximation (abstraction) and projecting the results back to the original graph (recovery), then refining the remaining local errors with GBP. We prove H-GBP convergence to the optimum by deriving the combined matrix operator of our abstraction and recovery steps and analyzing its spectral radius. Experiments on linear sparse graphs show that H-GBP converges fundamentally faster than standard GBP. Moreover, we validate H-GBP on two important spatial problems: Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) and Bundle Adjustment (BA). H-GBP markedly accelerates large-scale PGO and achieves state-of-the-art runtime across all tested BA scales.

</details>


### [10] [State-Aware Interaction MIL for Rare Joint Molecular Phenotype Prediction in Colorectal Cancer and Lung Adenocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06991)
*Dasari Naga Raju,Tripti Bameta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出State-Aware Interaction MIL，一种弱监督方法，保留生物标志物特异表征、建模其交互并监督完整四态分子配置，用于联合分子表型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合分子表型预测面临联合阳性样本稀少、不同分子状态组织学特征重叠的问题；现有方法单独预测各标志物（不建模交互）或将联合阳性当作二分类（丢失阴性及单阳性状态信息）。

Method: State-Aware Interaction MIL：保留各生物标志物特异的组织学表征，显式建模二者交互，并监督完整四态分子配置；基于冻结的UNI2-h与CONCH病理基础模型表征，在结直肠癌(BRAF+/MSI+)和肺腺癌(EGFR+/TP53+)上评估。

Result: UNI2-h下结直肠癌AP 0.5566(优于NaiveMTL 0.5161)，肺腺癌0.2784(优于IndependentPair 0.2525)；CONCH下结直肠癌0.4410(优于DirectJoint 0.3932)，肺腺癌0.1659(优于DirectJoint 0.1226)。

Conclusion: 病理基础模型表征含有罕见联合分子表型的预测信息；在结构化分子状态框架下保留标志物特异表征可提升从组织病理对这类表型的预测。

Abstract: Joint molecular phenotype prediction is complicated by small joint-positive populations and overlapping histological features across alternative molecular states. Existing computational pathology approaches typically predict biomarkers independently or formulate the joint-positive phenotype as a binary endpoint. Independent prediction does not model interactions between biomarker-specific histological representations, whereas binary joint prediction collapses the double-negative and two single-positive configurations into a single negative class. We propose State-Aware Interaction MIL, a weakly supervised method that preserves biomarker-specific histological representations, models their interaction, and supervises the complete four-state molecular configuration. We evaluate the proposed approach for joint BRAF+/MSI+ prediction in colorectal cancer and EGFR+/TP53+ prediction in lung adenocarcinoma using frozen UNI2-h and CONCH pathology foundation-model representations. With UNI2-h, State-Aware Interaction MIL achieved an average precision of 0.5566 in colorectal cancer (joint-positive prevalence 6.8%) compared with 0.5161 for NaiveMTL, and 0.2784 in lung adenocarcinoma (joint-positive prevalence 8.6%) compared with 0.2525 for IndependentPair. With CONCH, State-Aware achieved an average precision of 0.4410 compared with 0.3932 for DirectJoint in colorectal cancer and 0.1659 compared with 0.1226 for DirectJoint in lung adenocarcinoma. These results indicate that pathology foundation-model representations contain predictive information for rare joint molecular phenotypes and that preserving biomarker-specific representations within a structured molecular-state formulation can improve prediction of these phenotypes from histopathology.

</details>


### [11] [Learning to Curate What You Generate for Generalizable Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07008)
*Junhui Yin,Yuchen Yang,Yilin Yin,Shuai Na,Haoran Xi,Jianhua Yang,Muyi Sun,Man Zhang,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究基类样本也极少的G-FSCIL场景，提出利用可信合成知识进行稳定增量学习的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSCIL方法假设基类数据充足，当基类与增量数据都稀缺时会出现初始表示弱、语义漂移和边界不稳定问题；直接混合生成样本还会引入语义噪声。

Method: 先用冻结潜扩散模型构建类特定合成候选池，首次观测时做类反转并复用条件嵌入；学习知识筛选策略挑选语义一致且视觉多样的样本，并蒸馏为可迁移选择策略；再用合成数据设计合成先验原型初始化和双向边界校准。

Result: 大量实验表明方法持续优于现有FSCIL基线，减少遗忘并改善新旧类平衡。

Conclusion: 可信合成知识筛选与边界稳定增量适配能有效应对基类稀缺的G-FSCIL问题。

Abstract: Few-shot class-incremental learning (FSCIL) aims to learn novel classes from limited annotations while preserving prior knowledge. Existing methods typically assume a sufficiently large base session, but this assumption fails when both base and incremental data are scarce, leading to weak initial representations, semantic drift, and unstable boundaries. We study this underexplored yet realistic setting, termed Generalizable FSCIL (G-FSCIL), where the base session itself contains only a few classes. Although synthetic data can alleviate supervision scarcity, naively mixing generated samples often introduces semantic noise and exacerbates old-new boundary conflicts. To address this, we propose a framework that curates trustworthy synthetic knowledge for stable G-FSCIL. Specifically, we first construct class-specific synthetic candidate pools using a frozen latent diffusion model, where class inversion is performed at the first observation and the resulting condition embeddings are reused for on-demand generation. Building on these candidates, we learn a knowledge curation strategy that selects samples with both semantic consistency and visual diversity, and distill this process into a transferable selection policy during the base session, which is then reused without further optimization. Leveraging the curated synthetic data, we further design a boundary-stable incremental adaptation scheme, including synthetic-informed prototype initialization and bidirectional boundary calibration to mitigate old-new conflicts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing FSCIL baselines, with reduced forgetting and improved balance between old and new classes. Code is available at https://github.com/NiHaoWoJiaoYYC/G-FSCIL.

</details>


### [12] [DTFormer: Text-Guided Semantic Alignment for RGB-D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07014)
*Ziang Wei,Yinlong Liu,Yan Xia,Alois Knoll,Hu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DTFormer，一个RGB-D-文本三模态语义分割框架，通过文本引导的语义对齐模块引入语言先验，提升RGB-D分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流RGB-D语义分割模型主要依赖像素级监督学习特征，缺乏直接的高层语义约束。作者探究外部知识（如语言先验）能否为主流RGB-D分割模型注入更强的语义判别能力。

Method: DTFormer三模态（RGB-D-文本）语义分割框架。核心是文本引导语义对齐模块（TSAM），先将文本线索编码为一组语义原型，再在多个编码器和解码器层显式对齐多模态RGB-D特征与这些原型，从而对表示学习施加强语义正则化。

Result: 在多个基准上实验显示DTFormer持续带来性能提升，且保持简单高效。

Conclusion: 显式语义对齐是提升RGB-D语义分割的有效且实用的途径。代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: RGB-D semantic segmentation has made notable progress by fusing RGB and Depth, yet mainstream models still learn features almost exclusively from pixel-level supervision, lacking direct high-level semantic constraints. This raises a central question-can external knowledge such as language priors inject stronger semantic discriminability into mainstream RGB-D segmentation models. We present DTFormer, a novel tri-modal (RGB-D-Text) semantic segmentation framework. At its core is Text-guided Semantic Alignment Module (TSAM) that first encodes textual cues into a set of semantic prototypes and then explicitly aligns multi-modal RGB-D features with these prototypes at multiple encoder and decoder layers. This design imposes strong semantic regularization on representation learning, guiding the network toward more discriminative features. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that DTFormer delivers consistent gains while remaining simple and efficient. Our results demonstrate that explicit semantic alignment offers an effective and practical route to improving RGB-D semantic segmentation. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [13] [Anchor and Adapt: Asymmetric Prompt Adaptation for Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07016)
*Mengyang Zhao,Teng Fu,Haiyang Yu,Ke Niu,Bin Li,Xiangyang Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段提示学习框架Anchor and Adapt，将异常语义获取与目标正常外观适配解耦，在少样本工业异常检测中取得有竞争力的检测与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本工业异常检测中，少量正常目标图像不提供直接缺陷监督，异常提示难以从中学习；现有视觉-语言方法依赖人工构造的描述来提供异常语义，但这需要针对每个产品付出专门努力，且效果依赖提示选择。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段一从带标注的辅助数据中学习可迁移的正常与异常锚点（anomaly semantics）；阶段二固定这些锚点，用少量目标正常样本适配额外的正常分支。继承与适配的正常分支共同刻画目标正常性，并用文本锚点正则化鼓励与通用正常先验一致、与异常锚点分离。无需合成异常生成。

Result: 在MVTec-AD与VisA之间的跨数据集实验（1、2、4-shot设置）中，展现出有竞争力的检测与定位性能。受控消融实验评估了迁移锚点、非对称适配、双正常表示和锚点正则化的作用。

Conclusion: 该设计保留了已学习的异常知识，同时减少对类别特定异常模板的依赖，且无需合成异常生成。

Abstract: In few-shot industrial anomaly detection, the few normal target images provide no direct defect supervision, making anomaly prompts difficult to learn from these samples alone. Some vision-language methods therefore use manually specified descriptions to supply explicit anomaly semantics. However, constructing these descriptions requires product-specific effort, and their effectiveness depends on prompt selection. We propose Anchor and Adapt, a two-stage prompt learning framework that separates the acquisition of anomaly semantics from adaptation to target normal appearance. Stage I learns transferable normal and abnormal anchors from annotated auxiliary data. Stage II keeps these anchors fixed and adapts an additional normal branch using the few target normal samples. The inherited and adapted normal branches jointly characterize target normality, with text-anchor regularization encouraging consistency with the generic normal prior and separation from the abnormal anchors. This design retains learned anomaly knowledge while reducing dependence on category-specific anomaly templates, without requiring synthetic anomaly generation. Cross-dataset experiments between MVTec-AD and VisA under 1-, 2-, and 4-shot settings demonstrate competitive detection and localization performance. Controlled ablations assess the roles of transferred anchors, asymmetric adaptation, dual-normal representations, and anchor regularization.

</details>


### [14] [WiSPER: Pose-Supervised Predictive and Residual Flow Refinement For Multi-Person 3D Pose Estimation With WiFi CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07025)
*Gabriel Lee Jun Rong,Shanhong Liu,Pai Chet Ng,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Jamal Seyedmohammadi,S. Mohammad Sheikholeslami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WiSPER：两阶段框架，结合姿态感知掩码预训练与条件残差流精炼，实现基于WiFi CSI的多人体3D姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiFi CSI多人体3D姿态估计中，不同人的反射相互重叠、无法直接识别个体关节；现有掩码嵌入目标缺乏显式姿态监督，而结构化解码器保留坐标误差。

Method: 第一阶段PAMEL：将掩码隐变量预测与辅助姿态集监督耦合在同一CSI上下文，引导编码器从部分观测中定位关节。第二阶段ReFT：生成一组姿态候选以适配人数可变，并通过以粗坐标和逐关节解码器特征为条件的条件流对每个候选精炼。训练使用成对CSI与姿态标注，推理仅需CSI。

Result: 在PiW3D数据集上整体MPJPE为63.72 mm，较WiFi-JEPA降低40.0%；两人和三人场景分别降低42.1%和38.1%。姿态监督预训练优于纯CSI JEPA；启用残差精炼器使整体MPJPE在评估配置中降低13.8-15.6%。

Conclusion: 结合姿态感知预训练与条件残差流精炼能有效提升WiFi多人体3D姿态估计精度，推理阶段无需姿态标注。

Abstract: Multi-person 3D pose estimation with WiFi channel state information (CSI) is challenging because reflections from different people overlap without directly identifying individual joints. Existing masked embedding objectives capture wireless relationships without explicit pose supervision, while structured decoders can retain coordinate errors. We propose WiSPER, a two-stage framework combining pose-aware predictive pretraining with conditional residual flow refinement. Pose-Aware Masked Embedding Learning (PAMEL) couples masked latent prediction with auxiliary pose-set supervision on the same CSI context, guiding the encoder toward joint localization from partial observations. Residual Flow refinement with Transformer (ReFT) generates a set of pose candidates to accommodate a variable number of people and refines each candidate through a conditional flow guided by its coarse coordinates and per-joint decoder features. Both stages use paired CSI and pose annotations during training, while inference requires only CSI. Experiments on the PiW3D dataset show that WiSPER achieves an overall mean per-joint position error of 63.72 mm, a 40.0% reduction relative to WiFi-JEPA. For experiments with two and three people, WiSPER reduces MPJPE by 42.1% and 38.1%, respectively. Pose-supervised pretraining configurations obtain lower errors than CSI-only JEPA, and enabling the trained residual refiner reduces overall MPJPE by 13.8-15.6% across the evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [15] [Artemis: Geometry-Grounded Multi-Agent Driving World Models with Shared 3D State and Progressive Memory Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07031)
*Sitian Shen,Jiuming Liu,Mengmeng Liu,Yian Wang,Michael Ying Yang,Francesco Nex,Hao Cheng,Daniele De Martini,Ayush Tewari,Per Ola Kristensson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Artemis 是一种基于几何的多智能体世界模型，通过显式3D世界地图实现跨智能体的统一3D状态，并利用GeoAdapter区分非智能体的动态物体，提升多视角一致性与视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体视频世界模型依赖隐式跨注意力通信，缺乏显式几何约束和统一3D状态，导致多视角一致性差、难以恢复视线外的智能体；同时假设静态背景，无法表示动态背景。

Method: 从多智能体观测重建显式3D世界地图以统一跨智能体状态；提出动作引导的几何注入模块，渲染前景-背景控制图，并通过GeoAdapter注入扩散Transformer；对非智能体动态物体以其多帧历史位置为条件提供一致运动线索；使用渐进式视频滚动中的关键帧更新3D世界地图；并构建CARLA采样的MA-CARLA数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在生成视频的视觉保真度和跨视角一致性上优于现有方法；支持2D视频与3D点图同步多模态滚动，并可扩展到两个以上智能体及多相机场景。

Conclusion: Artemis 通过几何约束和显式记忆共享解决了多智能体世界模型中的一致性与动态背景问题，并展现了良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Recent video world models have witnessed the paradigm shift from single-agent to multi-agent involvements, which can reveal more complicated dynamics and cross-agent interaction in the real world. However, existing approaches commonly adopt implicit inter-agent communications via cross attention, which lack explicit geometry constraints and unified 3D state, thereby leading to poor multi-view consistency and struggling with recovering out-of-sight agents. In addition, most of them assume a static background, failing to represent uncontrolled background dynamics. To address these problems, we propose Artemis: a geometry-grounded multi-agent world model with explicit memory sharing. An explicit 3D world map is reconstructed from multi-agent observations to enforce a unified 3D state across agents, offering high cross-view consistency. Specifically, an action-guided geometric injection module is developed to simultaneously render decomposed foreground-background control maps, which are then injected into a diffusion transformer through a designed GeoAdapter block. Compared to previous methods assuming static-only background, our GeoAdapter can also distinguish uncontrolled non-agent dynamics, which are conditioned on their own multi-frame history positions to provide consistent motion cues. Keyframes selected from progressive video rollouts are used to progressively update the reconstructed 3D world maps. To effectively capture complex dynamic patterns, we curate a novel dataset sampled from the CARLA simulator called MA-CARLA. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our proposed method in terms of visual fidelity and cross-view consistency in the generated videos. In addition, our Artemis can support simultaneous multi-modal rollouts with both 2D video and 3D point map maintenance, scale to scenarios beyond two agents and multi-camera setting.

</details>


### [16] [Crop Yield Prediction for Punjab, Pakistan: A Tree-Ensemble and Leaf-Health Prototype, and What Random Validation Hides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07059)
*Amina Asif,Qurat ul ain Asif,Noor Bakhat Asif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了旁遮普省作物产量预测原型（RF/XGBoost/SVR/Ridge集成+MobileNetV2叶片健康分类器+SHAP网页），但审计发现高精度源于数据重复泄漏；在去重与独立FAOSTAT数据上，简单线性趋势反而优于所有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产量预测对农户和规划者很重要，但小规模农业数据表在新季节往往表现不佳。作者希望验证复杂机器学习模型在真实场景下的可靠性，并揭示常见数据陷阱。

Method: 构建随机森林、XGBoost、SVR及Ridge堆叠集成，配合MobileNetV2叶片健康分类器，部署为带SHAP解释的网页应用。通过数据审计（识别重复观测）、按年留出验证、FAOSTAT长表(1990-2024)、独立县级小麦面板及去重复叶片图像进行严格评估。

Result: Kaggle表随机80/20划分R2=0.991，但实为仅46个独立观测（年份连接复制9次）。按年留出后XGBoost从R2=0.994降至-0.20。去重及FAOSTAT上，单作物线性趋势(LOO RMSE 0.29 Ton/Ha)优于未给年份的模型(0.84-1.06)。县级面板显示变异主要为空间性，县级均值+共同趋势即可匹配模型。叶片分类器准确率99.87%，去近重复后召回96.4%，但因无图像-产量配对，健康分无增益。

Conclusion: 报告了复杂模型在数据泄漏和弱信号下的负面发现：高R2是重复观测造成的假象，简单趋势模型更稳健。叶片健康分因缺乏配对数据而无用，提醒小数据农业预测需严格验证和谨慎报告。

Abstract: Yield forecasts help planners and farmers decide on inputs, storage and imports, but small agricultural tables can make reported accuracy fail on a new season. We built a crop-yield prototype for Punjab, Pakistan that combines Random Forest, XGBoost, support vector regression and a Ridge-stacked ensemble with a MobileNetV2 leaf-health classifier, and deployed it as a web application with SHAP explanations. A random 80/20 split of a merged Kaggle-derived table (414 rows) gives the ensemble an R2 of 0.991. An audit showed that the table contains only 46 independent observations: a join with nine temperature records per year copied every crop-year nine times. Holding out whole years takes XGBoost on the same rows from R2 = 0.994 to -0.20. On deduplicated data, and on a longer FAOSTAT table (1990-2024, 70 observations), a per-crop linear trend (leave-one-year-out RMSE 0.29 Ton/Ha) beats every model not given the year (0.84-1.06). Neither pesticide use nor national temperature change explains the trend residuals. An apparent pesticide gain in the Kaggle table disappears on FAOSTAT, where the pesticide series is mostly imputed and the two releases disagree. An independent district-level wheat panel (36 districts, 13 seasons) shows that most variation in Punjab is spatial and that a district mean with a common trend matches the learned models. The leaf classifier reaches 99.87% accuracy on held-out PlantVillage images and recalls 96.4% of 336 unseen diseased leaves after near-duplicates were removed. However, no leaf image is paired with a yield record, so the health score used in the yield models had to be constructed and adds nothing. We report these negative findings together with the prototype.

</details>


### [17] [Decomposition-Guided Curvelet Thresholding for Sharp-to-Soft CT Kernel Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07067)
*Mahmoud Nasr,Jan K. Argasinski,Krzysztof Brzostowski,Adam Piorkowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合图像去噪框架，结合EMD、VMD、MEMD、BEMD等分解方法与曲波变换阈值处理，通过软/硬阈值处理后重建图像，在CT图像上验证VMD性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像去噪需在降低噪声的同时保留关键结构元素，传统方法难以兼顾。研究旨在通过融合多种分解技术与曲波阈值，提升去噪效果，尤其针对医学CT图像。

Method: 将EMD、VMD、MEMD、BEMD等分解技术分别与曲波变换阈值结合；对每个分解模态进行软阈值和硬阈值处理，再将去噪后的模态重组为最终图像。

Result: 在B50、B46、B41、B36等不同卷积核重建的CT数据集上评估，去噪效果显著提升；VMD在PSNR和SSIM上表现最佳，兼具噪声抑制与特征保留；软阈值保留细节，硬阈值去噪更强；方法在参考与非参考指标上均优于传统技术。

Conclusion: 该混合框架在医学图像去噪中具有优势，具备在医学成像领域广泛应用及未来与自适应阈值算法结合的潜力。

Abstract: Image denoising is a crucial task in image processing, focused on improving image quality by minimizing noise while maintaining essential structural elements. This study presents a hybrid denoising framework that combines several decomposition techniques, including empirical mode decomposition (EMD), variational mode decomposition (VMD), multichannel EMD (MEMD), and bidimensional EMD (BEMD), with curvelet transform thresholding. Each decomposition mode undergoes processing through both soft and hard thresholding, and the denoised modes are combined to rebuild the final image. Comprehensive evaluations of standard CT image datasets reconstructed with various kernels (B50, B46, B41, B36) reveal substantial enhancements in denoising efficacy. VMD consistently achieves the highest peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index (SSIM), signifying exceptional noise reduction and feature preservation. The study analyses the trade-offs between soft and hard thresholding: soft thresholding maintains intricate visual details, whilst harsh thresholding provides enhanced noise reduction. The suggested method surpasses traditional techniques in both reference and non-reference quality criteria, indicating its potential for broader application in medical imaging and future incorporation with adaptive thresholding algorithms.

</details>


### [18] [A BEMD-Based Quaternion Filtering Approach Sharp-to-Soft Kernel CT Image Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07071)
*Mahmoud Nasr,Jan K. Argasinski,Krzysztof Brzostowski,Adam Piorkowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BEMD-QBF框架，将锐核CT图像转换为类软核表现，在保持解剖结构的同时降噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT重建核选择存在权衡：锐核分辨率高但噪声大，软核噪声低但边缘模糊。需要一种后处理方法，无需原始投影数据即可改善图像质量。

Method: 利用二维经验模态分解(BEMD)将图像分解为内蕴模态函数，并在统一的四元数框架中结合四元数双边滤波(QBF)进行降噪与结构保持。

Result: 在B50、B46、B41、B36、B35、B31等多个重建核上评估，与Non-Local Means、各向异性扩散、双边滤波和QBF对比，SSIM和PSNR显示BEMD-QBF在结构保真度和降噪方面持续更优。

Conclusion: BEMD-QBF是一种有效的CT后重建图像增强方法，无需原始投影数据即可获得更优图像质量。

Abstract: The quality of computed tomography (CT) images is significantly affected by the selection of reconstruction kernels: sharp kernels improve spatial resolution but increase noise, whereas soft kernels diminish noise at the expense of edge clarity. This study presents an innovative enhancement framework utilising Bidimensional Empirical Mode Decomposition in conjunction with Quaternion Bilateral Filtering (BEMD--QBF) to convert sharp-kernel CT images into representations resembling soft-kernels, while maintaining critical anatomical structures. The technique disaggregates each image into intrinsic mode functions via BEMD and analyzes them inside a cohesive quaternion framework to attain efficient noise reduction and structural integrity. The proposed methodology is evaluated using several reconstruction kernels (B50, B46, B41, B36, B35, B31) and compared with recognised filtering strategies, including Non-Local Means, Anisotropic Diffusion, Bilateral Filtering, and Quaternion Bilateral Filtering. Quantitative evaluations of the Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) and Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) indicate that BEMD-QBF consistently attains superior structural fidelity and competitive noise reduction across all evaluated kernels. The results underscore the efficacy of the proposed strategy as a viable approach to enhancing post-reconstruction CT images, yielding superior image quality without requiring access to raw projection data.

</details>


### [19] [On Color Alignment in VAE Latent Spaces and Its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07072)
*Julian D. Santamaria,Kai Wang,Jesús Malo,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Alexandra Gómez-Villa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到图像模型的VAE潜空间中存在一个与亮度及对立色对齐的"颜色子空间"，并基于此提出三种应用：精确数值颜色生成、饱和度控制与颜色迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE是文生图模型的关键组件，已知能解耦数据中的主要变化因子，而颜色在自然图像中是最具结构性的因子之一（去相关后可得到一个亮度轴和两个对立色轴），因此颜色理应在VAE潜空间中成为独立因子。然而，现有研究对潜空间如何表示颜色仍缺乏探索。

Method: 通过对编码器做线性近似并进行有针对性的潜空间引导（latent steering），在从SD1.5到FLUX.2、Z-Image等广泛的VAE中定位并验证该颜色子空间。

Result: 该颜色子空间在多种VAE中一致存在；基于此提出的三项应用：ColorTuning在GenColorBench的细粒度CSS3/X11颜色系统上实现精确数值颜色生成的SOTA；饱和度控制可调整全局色彩强度；颜色迁移可将调色板匹配至参考图像。

Conclusion: VAE潜空间存在与亮度和对立色对齐的颜色子空间，这为精确、可控的颜色操控提供了统一基础，代码与模型已公开。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) are a key part of modern text-to-image models, which generate images within their latent space. VAEs are known to disentangle the main factors of variation in the data, and color is known to be one of the most structured of these in natural images: decorrelating it yields one luminance axis and two opponent-color axes. Color should therefore be expected to emerge as a distinct factor in the VAE latent space. Yet how these latent spaces represent color remains largely unexplored. In this work, we show that the VAEs of text-to-image models share a color subspace aligned with brightness and opponent-colors. Through a linear approximation of the encoder and targeted latent steering, we find this subspace consistently across a broad range of VAEs, from SD1.5 to FLUX.2 and Z-Image. Building on this characterization, we propose three applications: ColorTuning, which achieves state-of-the-art in precise numerical color generation on the fine-grained CSS3/X11 system of GenColorBench, saturation control, to adjust the global chromatic intensity, and color transfer, to change the palette to match a reference. The code and models are publicly available at https://julian075.github.io/Color_Subspace/

</details>


### [20] [Graph-Based Recognition of Simulated Train-Driver States From Facial and Upper-Body Keypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07083)
*Olivia Nocentini,Marta Lagomarsino,Gokhan Solak,Younggeol Cho,Qiyi Tong,Sara Zeynalpour,Marta Lorenzini,Alessandro Ledda,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB摄像机和图神经网络的列车司机状态监测系统，融合面部与骨骼特征将司机状态分为警觉、非警觉和紧急三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统如司控安全装置（dead-man switch）对警觉性检测有限且粗糙，无法有效应对司机疲劳对铁路安全构成的挑战，需被动、非接触的识别方法。

Method: 仅用单个前向RGB摄像头采集数据，采用图神经网络建模；通过消融实验比较三种输入特征配置（仅骨骼、仅面部、二者融合），并构建涵盖三种光照条件的受控RGB视频数据集。

Result: 在光照条件下，面部与骨骼特征融合的三分类模型准确率最高（81%），优于单一特征模型；融合特征在警觉/非警觉二分类中达99%准确率。

Conclusion: 面部与上身动态特征融合有效，研究向被动、非接触的列车司机状态识别迈进了一步，并公开了受控RGB视频数据集。

Abstract: Driver fatigue poses a significant challenge to railway safety, with traditional systems like the dead-man switch offering limited and basic alertness checks. This study presents a vision-based monitoring system that relies solely on a single front-facing RGB camera and a graph neural network to classify simulated train-driver states into alert, not-alert, and an emergency class comprising acted emergency-like behaviours. To optimize input representations for the model, an ablation study was performed, comparing three feature configurations: skeletal-only, facial-only, and a combination of both. Experimental results show that combining facial and skeletal features yields the highest accuracy (81%) for the three-class model under the light condition, outperforming models that use only facial or skeletal features. Furthermore, the combination of facial and skeletal features achieves 99% accuracy in the alert/not alert classification in light condition. Additionally, we introduced a controlled RGB video dataset containing alert, not alert, and acted emergency-like behaviours recorded under three illumination conditions. These contributions represent a step toward passive and non-contact train-driver state recognition based on facial and upper-body dynamics.

</details>


### [21] [A Data-Centric Review of Plant Disease Datasets: Taxonomy, Critical Analysis, Environmental Variability, and Implications for Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07087)
*Aamir Hilal,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Neeraj Goel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述批判性分析视觉与深度学习的植物病害检测方法，重点关注数据集问题，建立数据集分类体系，揭示实验室与真实田间数据的差距，并强调多模态（图像+环境参数）框架的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI进展迅速，真实世界的植物病害检测仍面临挑战。视觉与深度学习虽有效，但田间部署受限，关键瓶颈在于依赖缺乏环境多样性的实验室数据集，导致泛化能力差。而已有综述对数据集不平衡及环境变异性的探讨不足。

Method: 综述性研究：基于采集环境、可获取性、植物多样性、病害组成、类别结构与不平衡严重度建立数据集分类体系；对实验室与真实田间数据集进行对比分析；分析多层次不平衡（类内、作物间、跨数据集）及环境变异性的影响；探讨将温度、湿度、叶面湿度等环境参数与图像数据融合。

Result: 识别出阻碍病害检测的关键数据集差距：实验室与田间数据间的鸿沟、多层次数据不平衡、环境变异性不足，这些因素显著影响模型的性能与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 指出数据集构建、环境变异性、结构性不平衡、标准化及多模态病害监测等方面的关键挑战与未来方向，为精准农业的下一代多模态框架奠定基础。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in artificial intelligence, reliable real-world plant disease detection remains a persistent challenge. Visual and deep learning approaches have shown promising results, but their deployment under field conditions remains limited. A key bottleneck is the reliance on laboratory-generated datasets that lack environmental diversity, realistic backgrounds, and balanced class distributions, resulting in poor generalization. In contrast, datasets collected directly from agricultural environments capture natural variability and better reflect challenges faced by farmers across regions. This review presents a critical analysis of visual and deep learning approaches for plant disease detection, with emphasis on plant disease datasets. It establishes a taxonomy based on acquisition setting, accessibility, plant diversity, disease composition, class structure, and imbalance severity, and examines their implications for model generalization and real-world deployment. A comparative analysis of laboratory and real-field datasets identifies critical gaps that hinder disease detection. The review further analyzes how multi-level dataset imbalance, including intra-class, inter-crop, and cross-dataset imbalance, and limited environmental variability affect model performance and robustness, an area insufficiently examined in existing surveys. Beyond image-based approaches, it highlights the importance of integrating environmental parameters such as temperature, humidity, and leaf wetness with image data to improve prediction under dynamic field conditions. Finally, the review identifies key challenges, research gaps, and future directions concerning dataset construction, environmental variability, structural imbalance, standardization, and multimodal disease monitoring. It provides a foundation for developing next-generation multimodal frameworks for precision agriculture.

</details>


### [22] [MoonGS: High-quality Representation of the Lunar Surface via Gaussian Splatting Using Robust Depth Features from Image Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07110)
*Yun Jiang,Bo Zheng,Yingying Zhang,Xueming Xiao,Tao Hu,Hutao Cui,Zhiguo Meng,Ke Gao,Yang Gao,Meibao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoonGS，首个面向月球场景的前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，仅用两张稀疏图像单次前向即可生成逼真新视图，无需逐场景优化，性能显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球探测中稀疏巡视器图像的三维重建面临视角重叠不足、地表纹理弱、数据量有限等挑战，现有方法难以应对。

Method: (i) 自适应骨干设计，集成先进视觉基础模型提取鲁棒深度特征；(ii) 双语义先验融合：将语义线索与视觉特征结合优化高斯参数估计，并采用语义排序损失正则化背景深度；(iii) 熵引导启发式重采样，低成本选取信息量最大的远距离视角以增强稀疏观测。

Result: 在LuSNAR基准和自建弱纹理MoonBlender数据集上，较SOTA前馈NeRF/3DGS基线提升+4.9 dB PSNR、+0.29 SSIM，LPIPS降低40%，保持亚秒级推理；在VGGT等先进骨干上同样有效，嫦娥任务真实图像定性评估视觉质量最优。

Conclusion: MoonGS是首个面向月球场景的前馈3DGS框架，兼顾高质量与高效率，在合成与真实月球数据上均表现优异，具有良好的通用性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: High-quality 3D reconstruction of lunar terrain from sparse rover images is indispensable for autonomous lunar exploration, but remains challenging because viewpoint overlap is insufficient, surface textures are weak, and data volume is limited. We propose MoonGS, the first feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework tailored to lunar scenes. Given only two input images, MoonGS predicts pixel-aligned Gaussian primitives in a single forward pass and renders photorealistic novel views without any per-scene optimization. MoonGS (i) adopts an adaptable backbone design that seamlessly integrates advanced vision foundation models to extract robust depth features; (ii) integrates semantic priors in two manners: merging semantic cues with visual features to refine Gaussian parameter estimation, and adopting a semantic ranking loss that regularizes background depth; and (iii) employs an entropy-guided heuristic resampling strategy to augment sparse observations by selecting the most informative distant viewpoints with negligible overhead. Experiments on the LuSNAR benchmark and our synthetic weak-texture MoonBlender dataset show that MoonGS surpasses state-of-the-art feed-forward NeRF/3DGS baselines by +4.9 dB PSNR, +0.29 SSIM, and 40\% lower LPIPS while maintaining sub-second inference. Furthermore, we validate the broad applicability of our framework by demonstrating that it effectively leverages state-of-the-art backbones, including VGGT, to significantly boost performance. Qualitative evaluations on Chang'e mission imagery also show the best visual quality among compared methods, indicating robustness on real lunar data. The source code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/InRobots/MoonBlender.

</details>


### [23] [R2RI: A Multi-View Event and RGB Dataset for Robot-to-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07117)
*Gabriele Magrini,Riccardo Catalini,Federico Becattini,Guido Borghi,Pietro Pala,Roberto Vezzani,Lorenzo Seidenari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了R2RI，首个专门针对机器人-机器人交互（RRI）任务的大规模数据集，旨在推动自主代理交互的理解与建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人交互研究虽受关注，但因缺乏大规模基准数据集而进展受阻。理解自主代理间交互对协作系统、社交机器人及人机共存具有重要意义，因此需要一个专门的RRI数据集填补空白。

Method: 构建R2RI数据集，包含不同人形机器人及模拟真实人类社交行为的交互；提供自我中心（各机器人机载传感器）与外中心（外部固定摄像头）两种互补视角；数据量超650万帧、约5000个视频、120fps，涵盖事件与RGB两种域。并比较了各域及现有最优方法在多项感知与交互任务上的表现。

Result: 发布了包含所有任务和模态标注的数据集，并分析了各域（Event与RGB）的优缺点及现有方法在关键感知与交互任务上的性能。

Conclusion: R2RI为RRI研究提供了首个大规模基准，数据集与标注已公开，有望促进该领域发展。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling interactions between autonomous agents is a fundamental challenge in robotics, with broad implications for collaborative systems, social robotics, and human-robot coexistence. Although the study of robot interactions has emerged as a compelling research direction, progress has been severely hampered by the absence of large-scale benchmarks. In this paper, we introduce Robot-to-Robot Interaction (R2RI), the first dataset specifically designed to address the Robot-Robot Interaction (RRI) task. R2RI consists of different humanoid robots and realistic interactions modeled on real human social behaviors. Complementary viewpoints are available, \textit{i.e.}, an egocentric perspective from each robot's onboard sensors, and an exocentric perspective from external fixed cameras, thus enabling rich spatial and contextual understanding of the interaction dynamics. The dataset comprises more than $6.5$M frames and $\approx5000$ videos at $120$ fps, including Event and RGB domains. We investigate pros and cons of each domain, comparing state-of-the-art approaches for a number of key sensing and interaction based tasks. We publicly release the dataset and its annotations for all tasks and modalities at https://github.com/MagriniGabriele/R2RI.

</details>


### [24] [PlaySuite: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Interactive Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07127)
*Dheeraj Varghese,Anna Vettoruzzo,Walter Simoncini,Michelle Lorena Acevedo Callejas,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani,Kristof Meding,Joaquin Vanschoren,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PlaySuite，一个覆盖 5000+ 开源视频游戏的大规模交互式视觉智能基准，揭示当前多模态模型的"感知-行动鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基础模型评估多聚焦静态感知与推理，忽视了在动态环境中长时间执行行动这一智能核心能力，且缺乏跨异构游戏的可扩展评测手段。

Method: 从 PyWeek 与 itch.io 收集 5K+ 涵盖 Pygame、HTML5、Godot、Unity 的开源游戏（多为分布外）；构建面向 HPC 集群的统一闭环交互框架，并采用 Video-LLM-as-a-judge 协议将游戏里程碑映射为标准化进度等级。

Result: 评测 14 个最新开源模型（含 VLM、computer-use agent、VLA），发现尽管推理能力强，模型仍难以持续取得进展，在空间定位、动作执行和自我纠错上反复失败，呈现明显的感知-行动鸿沟。

Conclusion: PlaySuite 提供了可复现、可扩展的测试平台，用于衡量从视觉感知到目标导向交互的能力进展，并为开发能在动态视觉环境中行动、适应与泛化的模型奠定基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models yield strong performance on static perception and reasoning benchmarks, yet such evaluations largely overlook a central aspect of intelligence: acting competently in dynamic environments over extended time horizons. We introduce PlaySuite, a large-scale benchmark for evaluating interactive visual intelligence across more than 5K open-source video games curated from PyWeek and itch.io. Spanning diverse genres and engines, including Pygame, HTML5, Godot, and Unity, these independent games are largely out-of-distribution for current models, reducing the likelihood that success can be achieved by retrieving memorized walkthroughs or web-scale training artifacts. To enable scalable evaluation across heterogeneous titles, we develop a unified closed-loop interaction framework optimized for HPC clusters alongside a Video-LLM-as-a-judge protocol that maps observable gameplay milestones to standardized progress levels. We evaluate fourteen recent open models spanning vision-language models, computer-use agents, and vision-language-action models. Our results yield strong evidence of a perception-action gap: despite strong reasoning capabilities, current models struggle to make sustained progress and exhibit recurring failures in spatial grounding, action execution, and self-correction. PlaySuite provides a reproducible and extensible testbed for measuring progress from visual perception to goal-directed interaction, and a foundation for developing models that can act, adapt, and generalize in dynamic visual environments.

</details>


### [25] [CALR: Continuous Anchored Latent Reasoning via Render-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07175)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Bowen Jiang,Yanchao Hao,Wenchao Ding,Zheng Wei,Shaocheng Wu,Zhenjun Han,Jianbin Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CALR通过"功能锚定"统一潜在推理的形成与使用，解决连续潜在态推理内容坍缩、离散潜在态答案预测绕过推理的问题，在五个数学推理基准上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉潜在推理方法分两类：连续方法避开词表约束但推理内容会坍缩，离散方法通过量化提升准确性但答案预测往往绕过可恢复的中间推理。作者通过分析代表性系统，提出两个功能需求：答案必须依赖潜在态，且潜在态须携带有效的、问题特定的推理内容。

Method: 提出Continuous Anchored Latent Reasoning (CALR)，通过功能锚定将潜在态形成与答案使用相连。利用信息均衡压缩获得参考潜在态，CALR将潜在态中介的答案监督与推导级语义锚定耦合：前者让答案监督经由中间态路由，后者将其解码内容锚定到问题特定推导。并采用并行到自回归的课程，以生成前缀为条件发展顺序推理。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准、多模型族上取得显著准确率提升；在相同预算下，较可比连续潜在推理方法提升26.0个百分点。进一步分析显示其潜在态既支持答案预测，又携带问题特定的中间推理。

Conclusion: CALR通过功能锚定弥合连续潜在状态的推理有效性与答案可用性之间的矛盾，验证了潜在态可同时承载可依赖的答案预测与问题特定推理，为视觉潜在推理提供了兼顾精度与可解释性的方向。

Abstract: Visual latent reasoning compresses rendered derivations into compact intermediate states, reducing textual reasoning overhead. Existing approaches differ in how they represent these states: continuous methods avoid vocabulary constraints, whereas discrete methods improve accuracy through quantization into a finite codebook. Our analysis of representative continuous and discrete systems identifies two functional requirements: answers must rely on latent states, and those states must carry valid, problem-specific reasoning. Continuous latents influence answers despite collapsed reasoning content, whereas discrete latents retain recoverable intermediate reasoning that answer prediction largely bypasses. To address these challenges, we propose Continuous Anchored Latent Reasoning (CALR), which connects latent formation with answer use through functional anchoring. With reference latents from information-balanced compression, CALR couples latent-mediated answer supervision with derivation-level semantic anchoring: the former routes answer supervision through intermediate states, while the latter grounds their decoded content in problem-specific derivations. A parallel-to-autoregressive curriculum develops sequential reasoning by conditioning subsequent latent blocks on generated prefixes. Evaluations on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks across model families show substantial accuracy gains. Under matched budgets, CALR gains 26.0 percentage points over a comparable continuous latent reasoning method. Further analyses show that its latents support answer prediction and carry problem-specific intermediate reasoning.

</details>


### [26] [Deep Learning Based Illegal Bowling Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07223)
*Debopom Sutradhar,Niful Islam,Sudipto Mondal,Tasmima Hossain Jamim,Jubaer Muhammad Shufol,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于计算机视觉深度学习的板球非法投球动作检测方法。系统提取肩部帧与出手帧，分析投球臂角度变化，超过阈值（如15度）即判为疑似非法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 板球对投球动作有严格规定，非法投球动作影响比赛公平，但裁判难以实时监控。现有基于传感器的方案在实时比赛中存在局限，因此需要一种能在实战中实时评估的检测方法。

Method: 构建含62段视频、11名男性投球手（正手、背手、侧面多角度）的数据集。从投球视频中识别两个关键帧——肩部帧与出手帧，计算两帧之间投球臂角度的变化量，若超过预设阈值（如15度）则标记为疑似非法投球。采用深度学习计算机视觉方案。

Result: 在自建数据集上取得了较高的真正例率（true positive rate），表明系统在实时比赛场景中具有潜在有效性。但数据集以右手投球手和常规动作为主，需在更多样化环境和更大数据集上进一步验证泛化性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个用于检测板球非法投球动作的AI计算机视觉方法，展现了实时应用的潜力，但泛化性和鲁棒性仍需进一步研究验证。

Abstract: Cricket, often referred to as the "gentleman's game," adheres to a strict rule set for both batsmen and bowlers, where each delivery can significantly impact the match outcome. Detecting illegal bowling actions is crucial for maintaining fair play, yet it remains challenging for umpires to monitor in real time. Existing sensor-based solutions have limitations in live match scenarios, making real-time assessment difficult. This paper proposes a computer vision-based deep learning solution to detect illegal bowling actions in live cricket matches. To develop and evaluate our approach, we compiled a dataset of 62 videos featuring 11 male bowlers, capturing both legal and illegal bowling actions from multiple angles-front, back, and side. However, the dataset predominantly comprises right-handed bowlers with conventional actions. The proposed system identifies two key frames, the shoulder frame and the release frame from video footage of a bowler's delivery and analyzes the change in the bowling arm's angle between these frames. If the angle difference exceeds a predefined threshold (e.g., 15 degrees), the delivery is flagged as potentially illegal. We evaluated the system on a custom dataset and achieved a high true positive rate, suggesting the system's potential effectiveness in real-time match settings. However, further research is required to validate the system across diverse environmental conditions and larger datasets to ensure generalizability and robustness in various live match scenarios. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first AI-based computer vision method for detecting illegal bowling actions in cricket.

</details>


### [27] [Hybrid Cross-Modal Attention Network for Early Breast Cancer Detection in Low-Resource Clinical Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07243)
*Simon Hadush Nrea,Filimon Gidey Gebremichael,Gebrekirstos Hagos Gebrekirstos,Yaecob Girmay Gezahegn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合跨模态注意力网络(HCMAN)，融合乳腺钼靶图像与结构化临床数据，在埃塞俄比亚本地数据集上实现97.8%准确率，提升非洲资源受限地区乳腺癌诊断的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲乳腺癌死亡率高，因放射专业人才短缺与临床数据碎片化导致诊断延迟。现有深度学习模型多仅依赖影像且基于西方人群训练，难以适用于非洲医疗环境。

Method: 构建HCMAN，使用基于Transformer的跨模态注意力机制整合乳腺钼靶图像与结构化临床数据；在埃塞俄比亚四家转诊医院采集的1024名患者的2560张钼靶图像上训练与验证，标签经活检确认。

Result: 准确率97.8%、敏感度97.2%、特异度98.3%、AUC 0.987，显著优于仅影像基线。对低质量图像鲁棒，性能下降仅3.2%（基线为8.7%）。跨模态注意力显示对致密乳腺与年轻患者等模糊病例更依赖临床特征。轻量架构可在CPU上<2秒推理。

Conclusion: 该工作为非洲公平乳腺癌诊断提供了可持续、情境感知的AI方案，验证了跨模态融合在资源受限环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Breast cancer is the leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women in Sub-Saharan Africa, where delayed diagnosis results from limited radiology expertise and fragmented clinical data systems. Although deep learning models have demonstrated strong performance in mammographic analysis, most rely solely on imaging data and are trained on Western populations, limiting their applicability in African healthcare settings. This paper presents a Hybrid Cross-Modal Attention Network (HCMAN) that integrates mammogram images with structured clinical data using transformer-based cross-modal attention mechanisms. The model was developed and validated using a locally collected dataset of 2,560 mammogram images from 1,024 patients across four Ethiopian referral hospitals, with biopsy-confirmed ground truth labels. The proposed framework achieves 97.8% accuracy, 97.2% sensitivity, 98.3% specificity, and an AUC of 0.987, significantly outperforming image-only baselines. The system demonstrates robustness to low-quality images typical of resource-limited settings, with only 3.2% performance degradation compared to 8.7% for image-only models. Cross-modal attention analysis reveals clinically appropriate behavior: higher reliance on clinical features for ambiguous cases such as dense breasts and young patients. The model's lightweight architecture enables deployment on standard hospital workstations (<2 seconds inference on CPU). This work advances sustainable, context-aware AI solutions for equitable breast cancer diagnostics in Africa.

</details>


### [28] [What Words Keep of a Place: Zero-Shot Language Reasoning for Cross-View Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07269)
*Ayesh Abu Lehyeh,Jay Hwasung Jung,Safwan Wshah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究"仅靠语言"能解决多少跨视角地理定位任务：用MLLM将地面全景与卫星瓦片转成结构文本，无需训练即比较定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流方法依赖大量配对监督的联合嵌入，精度高但缺乏可解释性；作者想探究语言本身对该任务的贡献程度。

Method: 提示MLLM把地面全景和卫星瓦片分别描述为结构化文本，通过比较文本描述进行定位，全程不训练任何组件；在VIGOR 9826对数据、4个美国城市、三种设置下评测。

Result: 全池排序Recall@1仅0.39%（描述忠实但不具区分性）；缩小到10个邻近瓦片后，MLLM评判者评分使排序提升为随机的两倍并媲美强词法基线，且能指出一致/冲突字段；作为视觉检索器的重排器，在错误查询上图像重排23.5% Recall@1，而描述重排仅10.7%。

Conclusion: 语言能保留场景结构但丢失区分近邻地点所需的细粒度外观细节，可解释定位有潜力但依赖粗先验或视觉重排。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization is commonly solved as an image retrieval problem, matching a ground-level image against a database of satellite tiles through a jointly trained embedding. Such models are accurate, but they need large paired supervision and cannot show what evidence supports a match. In this paper, we study a different question: how much of this task can be solved through language alone? We prompt a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to describe each ground panorama and each satellite tile as structured text, and localize by comparing these descriptions. No component is trained. We evaluate on 9,826 VIGOR pairs from four U.S. cities, in three settings. First, the descriptions are faithful but not discriminative. They agree closely across the two views, yet ranking the full pool by description similarity almost never returns the correct tile (0.39% Recall@1). Second, we narrow the pool to ten neighboring tiles, as a coarse prior would do. The same descriptions now become useful: an MLLM judge that scores structural consistency doubles random ranking and matches a strong lexical baseline. It also states which fields of the two descriptions agree and which conflict, which an embedding distance cannot do, and which we see as a step toward interpretable localization. Third, we place the judge on a trained visual retriever. On the queries it ranks wrongly, reranking from images works, while reranking from our descriptions does not (23.5% against 10.7% Recall@1). Scene structure survives the conversion into language, while the fine appearance detail needed to separate nearby places does not. Code and prompts are publicly available at https://github.com/AyeshAbuLehyeh/GeoLingual.

</details>


### [29] [Localize Any Object in X-Ray Security Scans without Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07326)
*Yaqi Cai,Mingxuan Liu,Lorenzo Vaquero,Ning Wang,Nan Pu,Feng Xue,Elisa Ricci,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAO-X 提出一种自监督适配框架，用合成图像-标注对和粒度感知监督，让 SAM2 无需人工标注即可在 X 光扫描中定位任意物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X 光图像与 RGB 图像差异显著（颜色模式、边界模糊、体素叠加结构），导致 RGB 基础模型难以零样本迁移；同时 X 光标注稀缺且需专家，训练通用的 X 光原生态模型或微调 RGB 模型都很困难，数据规模化优势难以触及。

Method: 1) 显著性引导的 X 光物体挖掘模块分离多样物体实例；2) 在吸光度域进行物理引导合成生成图像-标注对；3) 采用遮挡控制的课程策略，逐步增加物体数量与重叠度，微调 SAM2 定位器实现适配。

Result: 在六个 X 光基准上，LAO-X 显著提升类别无关定位性能，在高度杂乱场景中相比 SAM2 和 X 光专用基线提升 2%~23% mAP，且完全无需人工标注。

Conclusion: 通过物理与显著性引导的合成及课程式自监督适配，可有效将 RGB 基础模型迁移至 X 光检测，实现无需专家标注的强泛化定位能力。

Abstract: Universal object localization in X-ray security inspection is critical for automated threat detection in safety-critical venues. However, unlike everyday RGB images that dominate web-scale visual data, X-ray scans exhibit distinct color patterns, ambiguous boundaries, and compositional structures caused by volumetric superposition. These gaps hinder the direct zero-shot transfer of dense perception foundation models trained on web-scale RGB data. Moreover, annotated X-ray data is scarce and requires expert labeling, limiting both the training of generalizable X-ray native models and the adaptation of RGB foundation models for X-ray data via fine-tuning. Given these challenges, the bright promise of highly generalizable perception models, enabled by data scaling laws in the RGB domain, remains largely out of reach for X-ray inspection. To this end, we introduce LAO-X, a self-supervised adaptation framework that Locates Any Object in X-ray scans using diverse synthesized image--annotation pairs with granularity-aware supervision. LAO-X first designs a saliency-guided X-ray object mining module to separate diverse object instances, which are then used for physics-guided synthesis in the absorbance domain. LAO-X further incorporates an occlusion-controlled curriculum strategy to fine-tune a Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) localizer, progressively adapting it to X-ray scans with increasing object counts and overlap levels. Experiments on six X-ray benchmarks show that LAO-X substantially improves category-agnostic localization, achieving 2\% to 23\% mAP gains over SAM2 and X-ray specific baselines in heavily cluttered scenarios, entirely without human-annotated labels.

</details>


### [30] [Compositional Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Hierarchically Grounded Semantic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07337)
*Chen Dai,Ganyu Zou,Nathan Self,Kevin Piper,Ramachandra Rao Seethiraju,Karthik Shyamsunder,Chang-Tien Lu,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HGSS：一种免训练的组合式概念擦除框架，通过层次化跨度定位与动态属性绑定解决文本侧路由失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的文本到图像扩散模型需移除受版权、不安全或用户指定概念。权重编辑法需逐目标重训练并修改检查点；免训练法虽部署友好，但在组合提示上存在文本侧路由失败：擦除目标可能通过相关类别被调用，其修饰词可能迁移到保留对象上。

Method: 提出HGSS框架，提升文本侧擦除的路由信号与编辑算子。其一，层次化跨度定位，通过词汇、分类学和语义证据解析擦除目标跨度，并防止宽泛上位词与复合中心词假阳性。其二，动态属性绑定，在早期去噪阶段用反事实参考和保留感知的交叉注意力目标精炼文本条件，保持存活的属性-名词绑定。不更新权重、不加学习参数。

Result: 在SEE上，层次化规避从29.54降至10.02，成对属性泄漏约减半，Neighbor E与AttrP达已报道方法最优。在UnlearnCanvas上，六指标平均略优于匹配的Semantic Surgery基线，达SOTA。

Conclusion: HGSS以免训练方式实现选择性概念擦除，在组合提示下有效抑制路由失败与属性泄漏，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Removing copyrighted, unsafe, or user-specified concepts from a deployed text-to-image diffusion model is now a practical requirement. Weight-editing methods can suppress fixed targets, but they require per-target retraining and modify the model checkpoint. Training-free methods, on the other hand, are deployment-friendly, but they suffer from text-side routing failures on compositional prompts. In such prompts, the erase target may be invoked through a related class rather than its lexical name, and its modifiers may migrate onto preserved objects. This paper proposes Hierarchically Grounded Semantic Surgery (HGSS), a training-free framework for compositional concept erasure. The framework lifts both the routing signal and the edit operator used by text-side erasure. First, hierarchical span grounding resolves erase-target spans through lexical, taxonomic, and semantic evidence, while guarding against broad-hypernym and compound-head false positives. Second, dynamic attribute binding refines the text conditioning during early denoising via a counterfactual reference and a preserve-aware cross-attention objective, keeping surviving attribute-noun bindings intact. HGSS selectively removes the erase target without updating model weights or adding learned parameters. On SEE, HGSS cuts hierarchical evasion from 29.54 to 10.02 and roughly halves pairwise attribute leakage, achieving the best Neighbor E and AttrP scores among the reported erasure methods. On UnlearnCanvas, HGSS slightly improves the six-metric average over the matched Semantic Surgery baseline, reaching state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [31] [A doctrine-grounded visual question answering dataset for Tactical Combat Casualty Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07339)
*Junseob Kim,Jade Chng,Ayman Ali,Victor Moas,Yichun Lee,Po-Chun Chin,Sunil Hwang,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TC3-VQA数据集，将公开TC3视频与权威文档关联，含581条目、11概念、1860个问题，覆盖干预识别、条令、临床推理、程序指导及视觉信息不足时的拒绝回答，答案保留原文与字符偏移，用于视觉语言模型在战术战伤救护的适配与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 战术战伤救护（TC3）需要将伤情视觉观察与临床指南关联，但缺乏将可见证据与可溯源条令相链接的监督数据，阻碍视觉语言模型的发展。

Method: 从公开TC3教学与实战视频及权威TC3文档构建数据集，结合视觉标注、段落检索、蕴含检验与跨模型家族验证；附带设备框、解剖标签、时间片段和来源元数据，并做自动化审计及两名医师、两名医学生的质量评分。

Result: 数据集包含581个条目、11个概念、1860个问题，条令类答案保留原文段落与字符偏移，88个保留条目附有人工评分。

Conclusion: TC3-VQA可用于视觉语言模型的TC3领域适配，研究视觉证据与临床知识的联系，并评估识别、条令记忆与拒绝回答能力。

Abstract: Tactical Combat Casualty Care (TC3) requires responders to connect visual observations of injuries and interventions with established clinical guidance. Developing vision-language models to support this process requires supervision that links visible evidence to traceable doctrine. We present TC3-VQA, a dataset constructed from public instructional and field TC3 videos and authoritative TC3 documents. It contains 581 items spanning 11 concepts, with 1,860 questions covering intervention recognition, doctrine, clinical reasoning, procedural guidance, and refusal when visual information is insufficient. Doctrine-based answers preserve verbatim source passages and character offsets. Construction combines visual annotation, passage retrieval, entailment checks, and verification across model families. Equipment boxes, anatomical labels, temporal segments, and source metadata accompany the question-answer pairs. Automated audits and ratings by two physicians and two medical students characterize annotation quality, with human ratings available for 88 retained items. The dataset provides a resource for adapting vision-language models to TC3, studying the connection between visual evidence and clinical knowledge, and evaluating recognition, doctrine recall, and abstention.

</details>


### [32] [Tracking Is Not Permanence: What Video World Models Keep of a Hidden Object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07355)
*Peng Xie,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究视频世界模型对不可见物体的表征能力。通过对冻结的V-JEPA 2进行隐藏物体实验，发现预测器对静止物体部分保留、对容器内物体完全不保留、对运动物体0.3秒内丢失，说明模型缺乏物体永久性，但该缺陷可通过少量训练廉价地修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型能追踪可见物体，但对其看不到的物体保留了什么信息尚不清楚。作者希望探究潜在预测模型是否具备"物体永久性"这一物理直觉。

Method: 遮挡V-JEPA 2预测器中的物体，比较其对隐藏区域的预测与编码器对两个仅在该区域不同的世界的表征；使用编码器自身的探针读取预测器输出；在合成容器场景上进行3千步预测器专属训练，并设置两个对照；评估IntPhys-2019基准及在操作和互联网风格视频上的持续训练。

Result: 预测器部分保留静止物体、完全丢失容器内物体、0.3秒内丢失运动物体（管状掩码下0.5秒，ViT-H在90%掩码率下1.1秒）；编码器以1.00读出物体存在、闭合容器内容可解码3.5秒，但预测器输出仅2%场景含球；训练可将该信念从0.05提升到1.00，IntPhys-2019从84.2%升至93.3%；持续训练在管状掩码下产生1.1-1.6秒运动物体延续。

Conclusion: 物体永久性在预测器一侧缺失，但该缺陷非潜在预测固有；训练可将其作为先验廉价地安装。VideoMAE几乎不保留任何信息，Cosmos保留静止隐藏物体但不保留运动容器内的物体。

Abstract: Video world models track objects they can see; we ask what they keep of objects they cannot. We hide an object from a frozen V-JEPA 2 predictor and compare its prediction for the hidden region with the encoder's representation of two worlds that differ only inside that region. The predictor's decision keeps a stationary object in part and one carried inside a container not at all, and loses a moving one within 0.3 s (0.5 s under V-JEPA's own tube mask; ViT-H keeps it to 1.1 s at pretraining's 90% masking ratio); in projection a trace remains, below the midpoint, at 14-60% of what a baseline copying the last view retains. The information is there: the encoder reads the object's presence at 1.00 and keeps a closed container's contents decodable for 3.5 s, while the predictor's output, read with the encoder's own probe, contains the ball in 2% of scenes once the box has been closed for half a second. On rendered scenes, permanence is missing on the predictor's side, and training installs it cheaply as a prior: three thousand predictor-only steps on synthetic containers take this belief from 0.05 to 1.00 against two matched controls. They also raise IntPhys-2019 from 84.2% to 93.3%, but so does a curriculum without containers, and which training habit the benchmark credits changes with its scoring rule. Continued training with tube masks produces 1.1-1.6 s of moving-object carry-over on manipulation and internet-style video, so the deficit is not intrinsic to latent prediction. VideoMAE keeps almost nothing, and Cosmos's next-token prediction keeps a stationary hidden object but not one carried inside a moving container.

</details>


### [33] [Identity-Conditioned Score Fusion for Open-Set Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07366)
*Manyi Yao,Jurijs Nazarovs,Eunji Chong,Abhishek Sharma,Rohan Sarkar,Yue Guo,Christian R. Shelton,Amit K. Roy-Chowdhury,Debashish Pal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出身份条件分数融合框架，无需训练即可为每个gallery身份定制权重，融合面部、步态、体型等多模态线索，提升换装行人重识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒行人重识别依赖面部、步态、体型等互补线索。现有自适应融合主要针对查询质量，但模型强度在不同身份间也存在差异，这点未被充分利用。

Method: 提出身份条件分数融合（identity-conditioned score fusion）：通过对比同身份一致性与跨身份冒名者，提取身份专属画像，结合查询条件自适应，用无参数规则融合；无需训练，保持分数校准。

Result: 在三个换装行人重识别基准上持续优于统计、基于排名及学习型基线，错误未识别率（FNIR）最多绝对降低8.8%。

Conclusion: 身份条件融合能扩大真假匹配差距，在开放集行人重识别中具有显著价值。

Abstract: Robust person re-identification often combines complementary cues such as face, gait, and body shape. While adaptive fusion typically targets query quality, model strength also varies across identities. We introduce identity-conditioned score fusion, a framework that tailors weights to each gallery identity without training. By contrasting intra-identity consistency against cross-identity impostors, it extracts identity-specific profiles that couple with query-conditioned adaptation via a parameter-free rule. This widens the separation between true and false matches while preserving score calibration. Evaluations on three clothes-changing person re-identification benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms statistical, rank-based, and learned baselines, achieving up to an 8.8% absolute reduction in the false non-identification rate and demonstrating the value of identity-conditioned fusion in open-set person re-identification.

</details>


### [34] [SimCortex v2: Joint Cortical Surface Reconstruction with Near-Zero Collisions and Self-Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07378)
*Kaveh Moradkhani,Sylvain Bouix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimCortex v2 是一深度学习框架，可从T1 MRI同时重建左右半球的WM与pial表面，兼顾拓扑正确与几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层薄且折叠紧密，从结构MRI重建皮层表面易产生网格自交、表面碰撞等几何伪影；深度学习虽将时间从数小时缩短至数分钟，但伪影问题仍未解决。

Method: 先从体积分割估计拓扑正确的初始表面，再用ribbon-conditioned、类U-Net网络预测多尺度平稳速度场，对四个表面联合优化。

Result: 在14个队列560例（13个为未见队列，年龄6-89，健康与临床人群，三家厂商扫描仪）上评估：平均对称表面距离0.253 mm，与最强基线持平；92.14%病例无表面碰撞，自交比例0.044%为学习类方法最低，而所有基线每例至少出现一次碰撞。

Conclusion: SimCortex v2 在保持高精度同时显著减少几何伪影，且公开代码、配置、预训练权重、预处理数据与评估划分。

Abstract: Reconstructing cortical WM and pial surfaces from structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is a prerequisite for surface-based neuroanatomical analysis, yet remains challenging because the cortex is thin and tightly folded. Reconstruction methods can produce geometric artifacts such as mesh self-intersections and collisions between cortical surfaces, and although recent deep learning methods have reduced reconstruction time from hours to minutes, these artifacts persist. We propose SimCortex v2, a deep learning framework for simultaneous reconstruction of the left and right WM and pial surfaces from T1-weighted MRI. SimCortex v2 estimates topologically correct initial surfaces from a volumetric segmentation and refines all four jointly using multi-scale stationary velocity fields predicted by a ribbon-conditioned, U-Net-like network. We evaluated SimCortex v2 on 560 cases from 14 cohorts, thirteen of them unseen during training, spanning ages 6-89, healthy and clinical populations, and scanners from three vendors. SimCortex v2 matched the surface-distance accuracy of the strongest baseline (average symmetric surface distance 0.253 mm) while showing no detected inter-surface collision in 92.14% of cases and the lowest self-intersection fraction (0.044%) among learning-based methods, whereas every baseline produced at least one collision in every case. Source code, configuration files, pretrained weights, preprocessed data, and the exact evaluation splits are publicly released.

</details>


### [35] [GeoWM: Efficient Direct World Modeling in Explicit Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07381)
*Mehrdad Noori,Guile Wu,Sam Hosseini,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWM：直接预测未来几何的世界模型，无需递归展开，用几何基础模型构建几何历史，条件化流匹配Transformer预测指定未来时刻的场景几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通过预测未来图像或隐表示再恢复几何，未显式建模几何结构，且依赖递归展开到长预测跨度，导致误差累积和计算成本增加。

Method: 利用几何基础模型将观测RGB帧转为几何历史，条件化流匹配Transformer，直接预测指定未来时刻的场景几何；引入轻量相机运动预测器估计未来视角，将观测几何投影到预测视角作为有效几何先验。

Result: 在城市驾驶、空中飞行、动态操作四数据集上，GeoWM在深度、相机位姿和3D场景几何预测上优于所评估的世界模型，且在更长预测跨度下大幅降低推理时间。

Conclusion: GeoWM以显式几何预测替代递归展开范式，兼顾精度与效率，验证了几何先验与直接预测策略在长时程未来几何预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Modeling 3D scene geometry and its evolution over time is essential for autonomous driving and robotics. A common paradigm is to use world models to predict future images or latent representations of the environment and subsequently recover geometry from these predictions. However, this paradigm does not explicitly model geometric structure and typically relies on recursive rollouts to reach longer prediction horizons, leading to error accumulation and increasing computational cost. To address these limitations, we present GeoWM, a geometry world model that directly forecasts future scene geometry at specified future horizons without recursive rollout.
